Queen at c5 attacks the whole c-file and the 5th rank and diagonals.
Queen at c5 attacks: c1-c8, a5-h5, and diagonals (a3-f8, a7-g1).
c7 is on the c-file. So Qc5 attacks c7.

So:
c8: attacked by Nb6 and Qc5.
d7: attacked by Nb6 and Ne5.
e7: attacked by Ne5.
c7: attacked by Qc5.
d8: not attacked.
e8: not attacked by Nb6 (attacks a8, c8, d7, d5, a4, c4), Ne5 (attacks d7, f7, c6, g6, c4, g4, d3, f3), Qc5 (attacks c-file, 5th rank, diagonals a7-g1, a3-f8).
Wait, does Qc5 attack e8?
c5 is (2, 4). e8 is (4, 7).
Difference is (2, 3). Not a queen move.
Is e8 attacked by anyone else?
No.

So the only legal move is d8e8.
----- END REASONING (Player_White) -----
